**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A. Reuse existing tracing spans: one span per turn carrying session_id, turn_id, message_id, repo_id, provider reason; SSE reader spans for snapshot/resync/lag. No new exporter or metrics registry (NFR5 decision stands).
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: dd2a7dd9
**Person Reply**: continue

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Decision**: Q4 — Security architecture: gate + authz ordering
**Options**: A. Shared EnablementGuard per surface,B. Axum middleware,C. Ad-hoc checks
**Unit**: skeleton-slice

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A. Per-surface via shared EnablementGuard helper — each RPC resolves enablement in-procedure (precedence flag_off > misconfigured > no_provider); each SSE handler runs session auth -> same helper -> ownership check (anti-enumerating not_found). oxpilot.status is gate-exempt and calls the helper to report state, not deny.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: dd2a7dd9
**Person Reply**: continue

**Unit**: skeleton-slice

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A. Single SELECT ... WHERE session_id ORDER BY seq per snapshot; index (session_id, seq); no cache tier, no pagination in U1. Delta ring buffer (capacity 256) serves mid-turn replay without DB reads.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: dd2a7dd9
**Person Reply**: continue

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Decision**: Q6 — Failure-domain isolation (blast radius)
**Options**: A. On request task,B. Own spawned task per turn,C. Global executor
**Unit**: skeleton-slice

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:15:58Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: B. Each turn runs on its own spawned tokio task (detach-to-complete). A panicking/stuck turn degrades exactly one session's active turn; orphan sweep reaps streaming rows to error on startup; broadcast lag degrades one reader to snapshot resync.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: dd2a7dd9
**Person Reply**: continue

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:35:39Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: infrastructure-design
**Details**: A. Zero new infrastructure — U1 lands inside existing api service (crate + SSE routes), oxidean-db tables on existing dialects, web route. No new Railway services, queues, caches, workers.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: 7e075fae
**Person Reply**: continue

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:35:39Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: infrastructure-design
**Details**: A. Reuse existing jobs — crate joins workspace rust lanes, dialect_oxpilot.rs joins db-dialects -E filter + TESTING.md table, route joins coverage manifests + check-route-sync. No new job/matrix so no Main ruleset change.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: 7e075fae
**Person Reply**: continue

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:35:39Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: infrastructure-design
**Details**: A. None — tracing spans only (NFR6); Railway platform logs carry stdout; oxpilot.status is the operator diagnostic; N=1 smoke ceilings in a stack test not monitoring.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: 7e075fae
**Person Reply**: continue

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:35:39Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: infrastructure-design
**Details**: A. Existing path: PR -> pr-<n> images on ephemeral Railway env -> squash-merge -> sha-<sha> -> staging; manual promote to prod. OXIDEAN_OXPILOT_ENABLED defaults off = zero-risk rollout.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: 7e075fae
**Person Reply**: continue

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-27T04:35:39Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: infrastructure-design
**Details**: A. Same path as every env var: .env.example + docs/CONFIGURATION.md documented, Railway IaC envs optional (default-off = absence valid). No secrets in U1.
**Unit**: skeleton-slice
**Message Id**: 7e075fae
**Person Reply**: continue
